<script>
/* 在原销量模型首次初始化前恢复本次真取数的新快照；旧来源资产留作审计。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const element = document.getElementById('monthTodaySalesDelta');
  if (!element) throw Error('缺少本次销量增量');
  const delta = JSON.parse(element.textContent);
  if (delta.schema !== 'muku.sales-sequence-delta.v1') throw Error('销量增量格式不支持');
  root.monthTodaySalesPacketProvider = function (text) {
    const original = JSON.parse(text), output = { ...delta.values }, copied = {}, inserted = {};
    for (const [key, operations] of Object.entries(delta.arrays)) {
      const previous = original[key];
      if (!Array.isArray(previous) || previous.length !== delta.baseCounts[key]) throw Error('旧来源数量不符：' + key);
      const rows = []; copied[key] = 0; inserted[key] = 0;
      for (const operation of operations) {
        if (operation.copy) {
          const [start, length] = operation.copy;
          if (!Number.isInteger(start) || !Number.isInteger(length) || start < 0 || length < 1 || start + length > previous.length) throw Error('销量复制范围不符：' + key);
          rows.push(...previous.slice(start, start + length)); copied[key] += length;
        } else if (Array.isArray(operation.items)) {
          rows.push(...operation.items); inserted[key] += operation.items.length;
        } else throw Error('销量增量操作不支持：' + key);
      }
      if (rows.length !== delta.targetCounts[key]) throw Error('新来源数量不符：' + key);
      output[key] = rows;
    }
    const expected = [...delta.targetKeys].sort().join('|');
    if (Object.keys(output).sort().join('|') !== expected) throw Error('新来源字段不符');
    root.monthTodaySourceModel = { ready: true, baseSha256: delta.baseSha256, targetSha256: delta.targetSha256, copied, inserted, meta: output.meta };
    return output;
  };
})(window);

</script>
<script>
/* 最新订单参考与原已核净量分源接入；不改已审批计划、库存和原补货测算。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  if (root.salesRefreshModel?.ready || root.salesRefreshModel?.runtimeReady) return;
  const node = document.getElementById('salesRefreshData');
  if (!node) throw Error('缺少最新销量数据包 salesRefreshData');
  const data = root.monthTodaySalesPacketProvider(node.textContent), meta = data.meta || {};
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const priorKey = cxOrderKey;
  const coverage = {
    native: { start: meta.nativeStart, end: meta.nativeReconciledThrough || meta.nativeStart, observedEnd: meta.nativeEnd },
    annual: { start: meta.annualStart, end: meta.annualEnd }
  };
  for (const period of Object.values(coverage)) if (!Number.isFinite(sqDateNumber(period.start)) || !Number.isFinite(sqDateNumber(period.end)) || period.start > period.end) throw Error('销量覆盖日期无效');
  const version = meta.version || [meta.asOf, coverage.annual.end, (data.annualRows || []).length, (meta.sources || []).map(source => source.sha256).join(',')].join('|');
  const rejected = [], mergeAudit = {};
  const sourceLabel = meta.amountLabel || '源销售额参考；Amazon含客户运费，非统一实收';
  const storeName = (platform, value) => sqStoreName(platform, value);
  const channelMatch = (row, filters = {}) => sqOrderScope(row, filters);
  function inScope(row, scope = 'business') {
    if (row.salesRefreshDataset === 'historical' && ['all', 'active'].includes(scope)) return srIsProduct(row) && pcIsIncluded(row.sku, scope);
    return srOrderInScope(row, scope);
  }
  function validRow(row, dataset) {
    const until = dataset === 'orders' ? meta.nativeEnd : dataset === 'historical' ? '2025-12-31' : coverage.annual.end;
    return row && row.sku && row.platform && row.store && row.orderId && Number.isFinite(sqDateNumber(row.date)) && row.date <= until;
  }
  function admitted(rows, dataset) {
    return (rows || []).filter(row => {
      if (validRow(row, dataset)) return true;
      rejected.push({ dataset, key: row?.pcOriginalKey || row?.orderId || '', date: row?.date || '', reason: '字段或截止日期不符，未接入' });
      return false;
    }).map(row => ({ ...row, pcDataset: dataset === 'historical' ? 'annualOrders' : dataset, salesRefreshDataset: dataset, salesRefreshVersion: version }));
  }
  function merge(previous, incoming, rule, dataset) {
    const start = rule?.start, end = rule?.end;
    if (rule?.mode !== 'replace_window' || !start || !end) throw Error(dataset + '缺少明确替换窗口');
    const kept = previous.filter(row => !(row.date >= start && row.date <= end));
    const map = new Map();
    for (const row of kept) map.set(row.dedupeKey || priorKey(row), row);
    for (const row of incoming) {
      if (row.date < start || row.date > end) { rejected.push({ dataset, key: priorKey(row), date: row.date, reason: '超出声明替换窗口' }); continue; }
      const key = row.dedupeKey || priorKey(row);
      if (map.has(key)) throw Error(dataset + '出现未闭合的重复商品行：' + key);
      map.set(key, row);
    }
    const result = [...map.values()].filter(row => dataset !== 'annualOrders' || row.date >= coverage.annual.start && row.date <= coverage.annual.end);
    mergeAudit[dataset] = { previous: previous.length, removed: previous.length - kept.length, incoming: incoming.length, result: result.length, start, end };
    return result;
  }
  const native = admitted(data.nativeRows, 'orders'), annual = admitted(data.annualRows, 'annualOrders');
  const historical = admitted(data.historicalOrders || [], 'historical');
  pcRuntime.orders = merge(pcRuntime.orders, native, data.replacements?.native, 'orders');
  pcRuntime.annualOrders = merge(pcRuntime.annualOrders, annual, data.replacements?.annual, 'annualOrders');
  cxData.support.orders = pcRuntime.orders; cxData.support.annualOrders = pcRuntime.annualOrders;
  cxOrderKey = row => row.salesRefreshDataset ? priorKey(row) + '|refresh:' + row.salesRefreshDataset : priorKey(row);
  for (const row of [...pcRuntime.orders, ...pcRuntime.annualOrders, ...historical]) {
    cxAllOrderMap.set(cxOrderKey(row), row);
    if (row.pcDecision) pcDecisionMap.set((row.pcDataset || 'annualOrders') + '|' + (row.pcOriginalKey || priorKey(row)), row.pcDecision);
    if (row.pcDataset === 'orders' && row.salesRefreshVersion) {
      const valid = typeof row.validOrder === 'boolean' ? row.validOrder : finite(row.valid) ? row.valid > 0 : false;
      sxData.orderValidity.entries[cxOrderKey(row)] = { validOrder: valid };
    }
    if (!sqChannelIdentities.has(row.sku)) sqChannelIdentities.set(row.sku, []);
    const identities = sqChannelIdentities.get(row.sku);
    if (!identities.some(identity => identity.platform === row.platform && sqStoreMatches(row.platform, identity.store, row.store))) identities.push({ platform: row.platform, store: row.store, key: row.platform + '|' + storeName(row.platform, row.store) });
    if (row.srProductName && !cxData.products[row.sku]) cxData.products[row.sku] = { sku: row.sku, productName: row.srProductName, sourceLabel: '最新来源精确SKU商品名' };
  }
  const business = new Set();
  for (const set of Object.values(srSets)) set.clear();
  srBySku.clear();
  for (const row of [...pcRuntime.orders, ...pcRuntime.annualOrders]) {
    if (srIsProduct(row)) { srSets.business.add(row.sku); business.add(row.sku); if (srClass(row) === 'nonstock') srSets.nonstock.add(row.sku); }
    if (row.srKind === 'unidentified') srSets.pending.add(row.sku);
    if (!srBySku.has(row.sku)) srBySku.set(row.sku, []);
    srBySku.get(row.sku).push(row);
  }
  for (const row of historical) if (srIsProduct(row)) business.add(row.sku);
  for (const [dataset, prefix] of [['orders', 'native'], ['annualOrders', 'annual']]) {
    srData[prefix + 'Products'] = pcRuntime[dataset].filter(row => row.platform === 'Shopify' && row.srKind === 'product');
    srData[prefix + 'Unidentified'] = pcRuntime[dataset].filter(row => row.platform === 'Shopify' && row.srKind === 'unidentified');
  }
  Object.assign(srData.meta, { commonNativeStart: coverage.native.start, commonNativeEnd: coverage.native.end, nativeObservedThrough: meta.nativeEnd, annualEnd: coverage.annual.end });
  sqSkuList.splice(0, sqSkuList.length, ...new Set([...sqSkuList.filter(sku => pcIsIncluded(sku, 'all')), ...business])); sqSkuList.sort();
  sqFacets.stores.splice(0, sqFacets.stores.length, ...sqFacet([...sqChannelIdentities.values()].flat().map(row => storeName(row.platform, row.store))));

  function getCoverage(dataset = sxState.dataset) { return coverage[dataset === 'annual' || dataset === 'annualOrders' ? 'annual' : 'native']; }
  function currentPeriod() {
    const source = getCoverage(), start = sxState.start > source.start ? sxState.start : source.start, end = sxState.end < source.end ? sxState.end : source.end;
    return { start, end, empty: start > end, partial: start !== sxState.start || end !== sxState.end, requestedStart: sxState.start, requestedEnd: sxState.end };
  }
  sxPeriod = currentPeriod; cxPeriod = currentPeriod;
  cxPeriodOrders = function () { const period = currentPeriod(); return period.empty ? [] : pcRuntime[sxDataset()].filter(row => row.date >= period.start && row.date <= period.end && pcOrderIncluded(row, cxState.sales.stockScope)); };
  sxCoverageNote = function () {
    const source = getCoverage(), period = currentPeriod();
    return (period.empty ? '请求期间未覆盖，不作0。' : period.partial ? '请求 ' + sxState.start + '—' + sxState.end + '；实际 ' + period.start + '—' + period.end + '，其余待核。' : period.start + '—' + period.end + ' · 美西业务日期。')
      + (sxAnnual() ? ' 历史订单数量参考；' + sourceLabel : ' 净量已核至' + source.end + '；' + source.observedEnd + '前新增订单另有参考，未核部分不作0。');
  };
  function windowRange(days, dataset = 'native') { const source = getCoverage(dataset); return { start: sxAdd(source.end, 1 - days), end: source.end }; }
  function sourceRows(mode) { return mode === 'annual' ? [...pcRuntime.annualOrders, ...historical] : pcRuntime.orders; }
  function aggregateRows(rows, mode) {
    let lower = 0, upper = 0, known = 0, upperKnown = true, amount = 0, amountKnown = 0; const orders = new Set();
    for (const row of rows) {
      const low = mode === 'annual' ? row.quantity : finite(row.netLower) ? row.netLower : row.net;
      const high = mode === 'annual' ? row.quantity : row.netUpper;
      if (finite(low)) { lower += low; known++; }
      if (finite(high)) upper += high; else upperKnown = false;
      const moneyEligible = finite(row.amount) && !row.cancelled && !row.technicalTest && !row.businessTest && (row.amountChartEligible === true || mode === 'annual' && row.amountChartEligible !== false);
      if (moneyEligible) { amount += row.amount; amountKnown++; }
      const valid = mode === 'annual' ? row.quantity > 0 && !row.cancelled && !row.technicalTest && !row.businessTest
        : !row.cancelled && !row.technicalTest && !row.businessTest && (finite(row.valid) ? row.valid > 0 : sxData.orderValidity.entries[cxOrderKey(row)]?.validOrder === true);
      if (valid) orders.add([row.platform, storeName(row.platform, row.store), String(row.orderId).replace(/^#/, '')].join('|'));
    }
    return { lower: known ? lower : null, upper: known && upperKnown ? upper : null, amount: amountKnown ? amount : null, known, rows: rows.length, orderCount: orders.size };
  }
  const windowCache = new Map();
  function skuWindow(sku, days, mode = 'annual', filters = {}) {
    const key = JSON.stringify([version, sku, days, mode, filters]); if (windowCache.has(key)) return windowCache.get(key);
    const range = windowRange(days, mode), rows = sourceRows(mode).filter(row => row.sku === sku && row.date >= range.start && row.date <= range.end && channelMatch(row, filters) && inScope(row, filters.scope || 'business'));
    const result = { ...aggregateRows(rows, mode), ...range, sourceRows: rows }; windowCache.set(key, result); return result;
  }
  function motionWindow() {
    const source = getCoverage(), selected = srSelectedPeriod(), end = selected.end < source.end ? selected.end : source.end, requestedStart = sxAdd(end, -29), start = requestedStart > source.start ? requestedStart : source.start;
    if (selected.empty || start > end) return { start, end, empty: true, partial: true, rows: [], count: null };
    const s = cxState.sales, q = s.search.trim().toLowerCase();
    let rows = pcRuntime[sxDataset()].filter(row => row.date >= start && row.date <= end && pcOrderIncluded(row, s.stockScope)
      && (!s.platform || row.platform === s.platform) && (!sxState.chartPlatform || row.platform === sxState.chartPlatform) && (!sxState.store || row.store === sxState.store)
      && (!s.sku || row.sku === s.sku) && (!sxState.stockClass || sxClass(row)?.stockClass === sxState.stockClass)
      && (!bxState.history || bxHistoryClass(row) === bxState.history)
      && (!q || [row.sku, row.shopSku, row.store, row.orderId, row.srProductName, cxGetProduct(row.sku)?.productName].some(value => String(value || '').toLowerCase().includes(q))));
    rows = ldApply(rows);
    const quantities = new Map(); for (const row of rows) if (sxValidOrder(row)) quantities.set(row.sku, (quantities.get(row.sku) || 0) + cxNet(row));
    return { start, end, requestedStart, empty: false, partial: start !== requestedStart, rows, count: [...quantities.values()].filter(value => value > 0).length };
  }
  if (root.spKpiModel) root.spKpiModel.window30 = motionWindow;
  const priorOverview = ovOverviewHtml;
  ovOverviewHtml = function (context) {
    const motion = motionWindow(), basis = sxAnnual() ? '历史订单数量参考' : '已核净数量下界', period = motion.empty ? '未覆盖' : motion.start + '—' + motion.end;
    let html = priorOverview(context).replace(/<div class="ov-kpi [^"]*" data-ov-kpi="codes"[\s\S]*?<\/div>/,
      ovKpi('codes', '近30天有动销SKU数量', motion.empty ? '未覆盖' : cxQty(motion.count), period + ' · ' + basis));
    html = html.replace(/<summary>近30天动销SKU怎么统计？[\s\S]*?<\/summary><p>[\s\S]*?<\/p>/,
      '<summary>近30天动销SKU怎么统计？<span>' + ovEsc(period + (motion.partial ? ' · 覆盖不足30天' : '')) + '</span></summary><p>按所选统计末日回溯30个自然日，保留当前平台、店铺、SKU和业务范围等筛选；' + ovEsc(basis) + '大于0的归并SKU去重计数。所选起始日期不截短此30天窗口；不足30天覆盖时仅显示已覆盖记录。库存与非库存SKU均按所选业务范围计入，不代表当前有货款数。</p>');
    if (sxAnnual()) html = html.replace(/商品销售额/g, '源销售额参考').replace(/商品行毛额 · 不含税运费/g, '源表金额 · Amazon含客户运费')
      .replace(/商品行毛额/g, '源表销售额').replace(/单均纳入商品毛额/g, '单均纳入源销售额参考');
    return html;
  };

  function selectDataset(value) {
    sxState.dataset = value === 'annual' ? 'annual' : 'native'; cxState.sales.period = sxAnnual() ? 'annualShipped2026' : 'near30';
    Object.assign(sxState, { chartPlatform: '', store: '', stockClass: '', bucket: '' }); cxState.sales.platform = '';
    const range = sxAnnual() ? getCoverage('annual') : windowRange(30, 'native');
    sxSetRange(range.start, range.end, sxAnnual() ? 'month' : 'week');
  }
  function updateDatePresets() {
    const dialog = cxEl('sxDateDialog'), select = cxEl('sxDateDataset'); if (!dialog || !select) return;
    const start = cxEl('sxDateStart')?.value, end = cxEl('sxDateEnd')?.value, source = getCoverage(select.value);
    for (const button of dialog.querySelectorAll('[data-srf-preset]')) {
      const value = button.dataset.srfPreset, selected = value === 'year' ? select.value === 'annual' && start === coverage.annual.start.slice(0, 4) + '-01-01' && end === coverage.annual.end.slice(0, 4) + '-12-31'
        : end === source.end && start === sxAdd(source.end, 1 - Number(value));
      button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(selected));
    }
  }
  sxDateHint = function () {
    const select = cxEl('sxDateDataset'), hint = cxEl('sxDateCoverageHint'); if (!select || !hint) return;
    const source = getCoverage(select.value);
    hint.textContent = (select.value === 'annual' ? '历史订单参考' : '已核净量') + '覆盖 ' + source.start + '—' + source.end + '；覆盖外待核，不补0。';
  };
  const priorDialog = sxDateDialog;
  sxDateDialog = function () {
    const result = priorDialog(), dialog = cxEl('sxDateDialog'); if (!dialog) return result;
    for (const button of dialog.querySelectorAll('[data-sx-preset]')) { button.dataset.srfPreset = button.dataset.sxPreset; button.removeAttribute('data-sx-preset'); }
    for (const [dataset, label] of [['native', '已核净量'], ['annual', '历史订单参考']]) {
      const option = dialog.querySelector('#sxDateDataset option[value="' + dataset + '"]'), source = getCoverage(dataset);
      if (option) option.textContent = label + ' · ' + source.start + '—' + source.end;
    }
    sxDateHint(); updateDatePresets(); return result;
  };
  root.addEventListener('change', event => { if (event.target.id !== 'sxDataset') return; event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation(); selectDataset(event.target.value); }, true);
  root.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('[data-srf-preset]'); if (!button) return; event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation();
    const select = cxEl('sxDateDataset'), value = button.dataset.srfPreset;
    if (value === 'year') select.value = 'annual';
    const source = getCoverage(select.value);
    cxEl('sxDateStart').value = value === 'year' ? coverage.annual.start.slice(0, 4) + '-01-01' : sxAdd(source.end, 1 - Number(value));
    cxEl('sxDateEnd').value = value === 'year' ? coverage.annual.end.slice(0, 4) + '-12-31' : source.end;
    sxDateHint(); updateDatePresets();
  }, true);
  document.addEventListener('input', updateDatePresets, true); document.addEventListener('change', () => Promise.resolve().then(updateDatePresets), true);

  const priorTrend = sxTrendHtml;
  sxTrendHtml = function (rows, options = {}) {
    let html = priorTrend(rows, options);
    const action = options.actionAttribute || 'data-sx-bucket';
    if (!/^data-sx-(?:detail-)?bucket$/.test(action)) return html;
    html = html.replace(/(data-fa-measure="amount"[^>]*>)[\s\S]*?(<\/button>)/g, '$1销售额（USD）$2');
    if (sxAnnual()) html = html.replace(/data-fa-trend-context=/g, 'data-srf-trend-context=')
      .replace(/商品销售额/g, '源销售额参考').replace(/商品行毛额/g, '源表金额')
      .replace(/金额为已知源表金额，未扣退款；Wayfair为供货额。/g, sourceLabel + '。');
    return html;
  };
  function decorateSales() {
    if (cxState.view !== 'sales') return;
    const host = cxEl('cxSalesBody'), subtitle = cxEl('subtitle');
    if (subtitle) subtitle.textContent = '销售参考至' + coverage.annual.end + ' · 净量已核至' + coverage.native.end + ' · 库存、利润及原方案沿各自日期';
    const option = cxEl('sxDataset')?.querySelector('option[value="annual"]'); if (option) option.textContent = '历史订单参考 · 至' + coverage.annual.end;
    const note = host?.querySelector('.sx-trend')?.closest('.cx-chart')?.querySelector('.cx-note');
    if (note && sxAnnual()) note.textContent = note.textContent.replace(/不含原生税运费/g, 'Amazon含客户运费，其他按各平台源定义');
    if (sxAnnual()) {
      for (const section of host?.querySelectorAll('.sx-trend[data-srf-trend-context]') || []) {
        const heading = section.querySelector('.sx-trend-heading h4'), measure = section.dataset.srfTrendContext === 'detail' ? root.faUiModel.state.detailMeasure : root.faUiModel.state.measure;
        if (heading) heading.textContent = measure === 'amount' ? '三平台源销售额参考（USD） · Amazon含客户运费' : '三平台历史订单数量参考';
      }
      const pieNote = host?.querySelector('.sx-pies .cx-note');
      if (pieNote) pieNote.textContent = pieNote.textContent.replace('销售额为商品行毛额，不含原生税运费、未扣退款', '源销售额按各平台原表定义；Amazon含客户运费、未扣退款');
      for (const heading of host?.querySelectorAll('th') || []) if (/商品.*销售额USD/.test(heading.textContent)) heading.textContent = heading.textContent.replace(/商品.*销售额USD/, '源销售额参考USD');
    }
    const overview = host?.querySelector('.ov-overview');
    if (overview && !overview.querySelector('.srf-source-note')) overview.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<p class="cx-caption srf-source-note">最新订单参考至' + cxEsc(coverage.annual.end) + '；净量已核至' + cxEsc(coverage.native.end) + '。' + (sxAnnual() ? cxEsc(sourceLabel) : '新增未核日期不作0销量。') + '</p>');
    for (const heading of host?.querySelectorAll('th') || []) if (heading.textContent === '近30／60净套') heading.textContent = '近30／60净量 · 至' + coverage.native.end;
  }
  const priorSales = cxRenderSales; cxRenderSales = function () { const result = priorSales(); decorateSales(); return result; };
  const priorView = setPageView; setPageView = function (view) { const result = priorView(view); decorateSales(); return result; };
  const priorOverviewRender = ovRenderOverview; ovRenderOverview = function () { const result = priorOverviewRender(); decorateSales(); return result; };
  const priorOrderTable = cxOrderTable;
  cxOrderTable = function () { const html = priorOrderTable.apply(this, arguments); return sxAnnual() ? html.replace(/商品总销售额USD/g, '源销售额参考USD（Amazon含客户运费）') : html; };
  const priorDownload = cxDownloadCsv;
  cxDownloadCsv = function (filename, headers, rows) {
    const queryAnnual = String(filename).startsWith('SKU数据查询_') && root.salesRefreshModel?.query?.resolveMode(sqUiState.filters) === 'annual';
    const salesAnnual = /(?:商品业务|SKU销量)/.test(String(filename)) && sxAnnual();
    if (queryAnnual || salesAnnual) headers = headers.map(label => /商品.*销售额|商品金额/.test(label) ? '源销售额参考USD（Amazon含客户运费）' : label);
    return priorDownload(filename, headers, rows);
  };
  if (typeof srRefreshStockSales === 'function') srRefreshStockSales();
  if (typeof sqSalesCache !== 'undefined') sqSalesCache.clear();
  if (typeof cfAgeCache !== 'undefined') cfAgeCache.clear();
  const model = { ready: false, runtimeReady: true, data, meta, version, coverage, historical, business, mergeAudit, rejected,
    getCoverage, currentPeriod, windowRange, sourceRows, channelMatch, inScope, aggregateRows, skuWindow, motionWindow, storeName, decorateSales, sourceLabel };
  model.finish = function () {
    const dataset = coverage.native.end >= coverage.annual.end ? 'native' : 'annual', range = windowRange(30, dataset);
    sxState.dataset = dataset; cxState.sales.period = dataset === 'annual' ? 'annualShipped2026' : 'near30';
    Object.assign(sxState, { start: range.start, end: range.end, bucket: '', grain: 'week', chartPlatform: '', store: '' });
    if (cxState.view === 'sales') cxRenderSales(); model.ready = true;
  };
  root.salesRefreshModel = model;
})(window);

</script>
<script>
/* 查询使用本轮独立版本cache，旧查询闭包只保留价格、利润和库存构建。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const core = root.salesRefreshModel;
  if (!core?.runtimeReady) throw Error('销量运行时未初始化');
  const previous = root.spQueryPeriodModel, priorBuild = sqBuildRow;
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const aggregateCache = new Map(), periodCache = new Map();
  const labels = { native: '已核净销量（下界）', annual: '历史订单数量参考' };
  const names = new Map();
  for (const row of [...pcRuntime.orders, ...pcRuntime.annualOrders, ...core.historical]) if (row.srProductName) {
    const old = names.get(row.sku); if (!old || old.date < row.date) names.set(row.sku, { date: row.date, name: row.srProductName });
  }
  // 原UI闭包也持有此对象，修改对象字段可同步其说明；业务聚合完全走新cache。
  if (previous?.coverage) for (const key of ['native', 'annual']) Object.assign(previous.coverage[key], core.coverage[key]);
  function defaults() { return { ...previous.defaults(), ...core.windowRange(30, 'annual'), salesMode: 'annual', scope: 'business' }; }
  function resolveMode(filters) { return ['native', 'annual'].includes(filters.salesMode) ? filters.salesMode
    : filters.start >= core.coverage.native.start && filters.end <= core.coverage.native.end ? 'native' : 'annual'; }
  function cacheKey(filters) { return JSON.stringify([core.version, resolveMode(filters), filters.start, filters.end, filters.platform || '', filters.store || '', filters.scope || 'business']); }
  function periodCoverage(filters) {
    const key = cacheKey(filters); if (periodCache.has(key)) return periodCache.get(key);
    const mode = resolveMode(filters), source = core.coverage[mode], start = filters.start, end = filters.end;
    const valid = Number.isFinite(sqDateNumber(start)) && Number.isFinite(sqDateNumber(end)) && start <= end;
    const actualStart = valid ? start > source.start ? start : source.start : null;
    const actualEnd = valid ? end < source.end ? end : source.end : null;
    const overlap = valid && actualStart <= actualEnd;
    const older = mode === 'annual' && valid ? core.historical.filter(row => row.date >= start && row.date <= end && core.channelMatch(row, filters) && core.inScope(row, filters.scope)) : [];
    const dates = older.map(row => row.date).sort(), complete = Boolean(valid && start >= source.start && end <= source.end);
    let note = !overlap && !dates.length ? '请求期间未覆盖，不作0销量' : complete ? '请求 ' + start + '—' + end + '；' + labels[mode] + '，来源日期完整覆盖'
      : '已覆盖部分：' + (overlap ? actualStart + '—' + actualEnd : '无完整日期段') + (dates.length ? '；另有历史记录小计，完整覆盖待核' : '') + '；完整请求期间上界待核';
    if (mode === 'native') note += '；净量已核至' + source.end + '，新增订单参考至' + core.coverage.annual.end + '，未核日期不补0';
    else note += '；' + core.sourceLabel;
    const result = { mode, label: labels[mode], requestedStart: start, requestedEnd: end, sourceStart: source.start, sourceEnd: source.end,
      actualStart: overlap ? actualStart : null, actualEnd: overlap ? actualEnd : null, periodComplete: complete, hasOverlap: Boolean(overlap || dates.length),
      historicalRecordStart: dates[0] || null, historicalRecordEnd: dates.at(-1) || null, note };
    periodCache.set(key, result); return result;
  }
  function salesMap(filters) {
    const key = cacheKey(filters); if (aggregateCache.has(key)) return aggregateCache.get(key);
    const mode = resolveMode(filters), period = periodCoverage(filters), groups = new Map();
    for (const row of core.sourceRows(mode)) {
      if (row.date < filters.start || row.date > filters.end || !core.channelMatch(row, filters) || !core.inScope(row, filters.scope)) continue;
      if (mode === 'native' && (row.date < core.coverage.native.start || row.date > core.coverage.native.end)) continue;
      if (!groups.has(row.sku)) groups.set(row.sku, []); groups.get(row.sku).push(row);
    }
    const values = new Map([...groups].map(([sku, rows]) => [sku, { ...core.aggregateRows(rows, mode), sourceRows: rows }]));
    aggregateCache.set(key, values); return values;
  }
  function salesFor(sku, filters) {
    const period = periodCoverage(filters), value = salesMap(filters).get(sku);
    const zero = period.mode === 'native' && period.periodComplete && !value && sqStockBySku.get(sku)?.salesCoverage?.coverage60 === true;
    const known = value?.known > 0 || zero, units = period.hasOverlap && known ? value?.lower ?? 0 : null;
    const excludedZero = value?.lower === 0 && value.sourceRows.every(row => row.cancelled || row.technicalTest || row.businessTest);
    return { units, unitsUpper: period.periodComplete && known ? value?.upper ?? (zero ? 0 : null) : null,
      orders: period.hasOverlap && known ? value?.orderCount ?? 0 : null,
      salesAmount: period.hasOverlap ? value?.amount ?? (zero || excludedZero ? 0 : null) : null,
      salesKnown: period.periodComplete && known, rows: value?.sourceRows || [], period,
      note: period.note + (!value && !zero ? '；此SKU未见该期间计量证据，保留待核' : '') };
  }
  sqSalesMap = function (filters, start = filters.start, end = filters.end) { return salesMap({ ...filters, start, end }); };
  sqSalesValue = function (sku, filters, start = filters.start, end = filters.end) { return salesFor(sku, { ...filters, start, end,
    salesMode: arguments.length > 2 ? 'native' : filters.salesMode || 'auto' }); };
  sqBuildRow = function (sku, filters) {
    // 以库存历史作用域取原价格/利润，避免旧销量cache对新采购属性作错误清空；下方再按当前属性置不适用。
    const row = priorBuild(sku, { ...filters, scope: 'all' }), sales = salesFor(sku, filters), period = sales.period;
    const recent = days => salesFor(sku, { ...filters, ...core.windowRange(days, 'native'), salesMode: 'native' });
    const near30 = recent(30), near60 = recent(60), classes = [...new Set(sales.rows.map(srClass))];
    const nonstock = filters.scope === 'nonstock' || classes.length > 0 && classes.every(key => key === 'nonstock');
    return { ...row, name: row.name || names.get(sku)?.name || '', units: sales.units, unitsUpper: sales.unitsUpper, orders: sales.orders, salesAmount: sales.salesAmount,
      salesKnown: sales.salesKnown, salesMode: period.mode, salesBasis: period.label, periodStart: filters.start, periodEnd: filters.end,
      actualCoverageStart: period.actualStart, actualCoverageEnd: period.actualEnd, historicalRecordStart: period.historicalRecordStart, historicalRecordEnd: period.historicalRecordEnd,
      periodComplete: period.periodComplete, coverageNote: sales.note, _salesRows: sales.rows, _period: period, businessClasses: classes,
      net30: near30.units, net30Upper: near30.unitsUpper, net60: near60.units, net60Upper: near60.unitsUpper,
      nearSalesEnd: core.coverage.native.end, nearSalesBasis: labels.native, active: pcIsIncluded(sku, 'active'),
      ...(nonstock ? { available: null, reserved: null, transit: null, stockWarehouses: [], stockDate: '非库存业务，不套库存余额', ageText: '不适用：非库存业务', ageSort: null, ageSets: null,
        displayPrice: null, priceItem: null, shipping: null, priceTotal: null, operatingMargin: null, realMargin: null, _reference: null, referenceKey: '', profitBasis: 'missing',
        profitStatus: '非库存业务，不借库存利润参考', sourceNote: '按本期已证订单采购属性，不套库存利润' } : {}) };
  };
  sqMatches = function (row, filters) {
    const scope = filters.scope || 'business';
    if (scope === 'nonstock' ? !salesMap(filters).has(row.sku) : scope === 'business' ? !(pcIsIncluded(row.sku, 'all') || core.business.has(row.sku)) : !pcIsIncluded(row.sku, scope)) return false;
    const q = String(filters.search || '').trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q && ![row.sku, row.name, row.platform, row.store, row.warehouse].some(value => String(value || '').toLowerCase().includes(q))) return false;
    if (filters.productType && filters.productType !== row.productType) return false;
    if ((sqValues(filters.platform).length || sqValues(filters.store).length) && !(sqChannelIdentities.get(row.sku) || []).some(identity => core.channelMatch(identity, filters))) return false;
    if (sqValues(filters.warehouse).length && !sqValues(filters.warehouse).map(sqWarehouseCode).some(value => row.stockWarehouses.includes(value))) return false;
    if (filters.ageBand && !(sqStockBySku.get(row.sku)?.ageBands?.[filters.ageBand] > 0)) return false;
    if (sqLimit(filters.salesMin) != null && (!finite(row.units) || row.units < Number(filters.salesMin))) return false;
    if (sqLimit(filters.salesMax) != null && (!row.periodComplete || !finite(row.unitsUpper) || row.unitsUpper > Number(filters.salesMax))) return false;
    const margin = filters.marginBasis === 'real' ? row.realMargin : row.operatingMargin;
    if (filters.evidenceMode === 'missing' && finite(margin)) return false;
    return sqInRange(margin, sqLimit(filters.marginMin) == null ? null : Number(filters.marginMin) / 100, sqLimit(filters.marginMax) == null ? null : Number(filters.marginMax) / 100)
      && sqInRange(row.available, filters.availableMin, filters.availableMax) && sqInRange(row.displayPrice, filters.priceMin, filters.priceMax);
  };
  function getRows(filters = {}, sort = { key: 'units', direction: 'desc' }) {
    const f = { ...defaults(), ...filters }; if (!Number.isFinite(sqDateNumber(f.start)) || !Number.isFinite(sqDateNumber(f.end)) || f.start > f.end) return [];
    // 无搜索只列当前库存人口和本期有据业务商品码；显式搜索可查完整历史身份。
    const searched = Boolean(String(f.search || '').trim()), scope = f.scope || 'business';
    const candidates = !searched && scope === 'business' ? new Set([...sqSkuList.filter(sku => pcIsIncluded(sku, 'active')), ...salesMap(f).keys()])
      : scope === 'nonstock' ? new Set(salesMap(f).keys()) : sqSkuList;
    return sqSortRows([...candidates].map(sku => sqBuildRow(sku, f)).filter(row => sqMatches(row, f)), sort);
  }
  sqExportRows = getRows;
  // 沿用原导出字段及仓储模拟链，只把导出人口和顺序约束到当前查询结果。
  const priorExport = sqUiExport;
  sqUiExport = function () {
    const wanted = getRows(sqUiState.filters, sqUiState.sort), download = cxDownloadCsv;
    cxDownloadCsv = function (filename, headers, rows) {
      const skuColumn = headers.indexOf('商品SKU');
      if (String(filename).startsWith('SKU数据查询_') && skuColumn >= 0) {
        const bySku = new Map(rows.map(row => [row[skuColumn], row]));
        rows = wanted.map(row => bySku.get(row.sku));
        if (rows.some(row => !row)) throw Error('查询与导出人口不一致');
      }
      return download(filename, headers, rows);
    };
    try { return priorExport(); } finally { cxDownloadCsv = download; }
  };
  sqQuery = function (filters, sort, page = 0, size = 50) {
    const f = { ...defaults(), ...filters }, rows = getRows(f, sort), period = periodCoverage(f), known = rows.filter(row => finite(row.units)), amount = rows.filter(row => finite(row.salesAmount));
    sqActiveRows = rows;
    return { rows: rows.slice(page * size, (page + 1) * size), total: rows.length, facets: sqFacets,
      summary: { skuCount: rows.length, units: known.length ? known.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.units, 0) : null, unitsKnownCount: known.length,
        salesAmount: amount.length ? amount.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.salesAmount, 0) : null, salesAmountKnownCount: amount.length,
        operatingMarginKnownCount: rows.filter(row => finite(row.operatingMargin)).length, profitMissingCount: rows.filter(row => !finite(row.operatingMargin)).length },
      meta: { salesCutoff: core.coverage.native.end, salesBasis: period.label, salesCoverage: period.note, period,
        productTypeRule: '库存分类沿已核身份；非库存属性按订单商品行区分。', message: '销售参考至' + core.coverage.annual.end + '；净量及近30/60天已核至' + core.coverage.native.end } };
  };
  sqUiRecentRange = days => core.windowRange(days, 'native');
  const priorOrderHtml = sqOrdersHtml;
  sqOrdersHtml = function (row) { return priorOrderHtml(row).replace(/商品金额USD/g, row.salesMode === 'annual' ? '源销售额参考USD（Amazon含客户运费）' : '商品金额USD'); };
  const priorSummary = sqUiSummary;
  sqUiSummary = function (result) {
    const annual = result.meta.period.mode === 'annual';
    return priorSummary(result).replace('款SKU</span>', '个业务商品码</span>')
      .replace('商品销售额', annual ? '源销售额参考' : '商品销售额')
      + '<p class="cx-caption">业务商品码含当前经营库存SKU及所选期间有记录的商品码；当前库存人口未扩大。搜索可查历史商品身份。' + (annual ? core.sourceLabel : '') + '</p>';
  };
  const priorRender = sqRenderQuery;
  sqRenderQuery = function () {
    sqUiColumnLabels.salesAmount = resolveMode(sqUiState.filters) === 'annual' ? '源销售额参考USD' : '商品总销售额';
    const result = priorRender(), host = cxEl('sqQueryBody');
    for (const button of host?.querySelectorAll('[data-sq-window]') || []) { button.textContent = '净量' + button.dataset.sqWindow + '天'; button.title = '已核至' + core.coverage.native.end; }
    const intro = host?.querySelector('.sq-intro>p'); if (intro) intro.textContent = '历史订单参考至' + core.coverage.annual.end + ' · 净量及近30/60天已核至' + core.coverage.native.end;
    return result;
  };
  root.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('#sqQueryBody [data-sq-reset],#sqQueryBody [data-sq-preset],#sqQueryBody [data-sq-window],#sqQueryBody [data-sq-remove]'); if (!button) return;
    event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation(); sqUiReadDraft();
    if (button.hasAttribute('data-sq-reset')) sqUiState.draft = sqUiState.filters = { ...defaults() };
    else if (button.hasAttribute('data-sq-remove')) {
      const key = button.dataset.sqRemove, next = defaults();
      if (key === 'dates') Object.assign(sqUiState.filters, { start: next.start, end: next.end, salesMode: next.salesMode });
      else sqUiState.filters[key] = next[key] ?? '';
      sqUiState.draft = { ...sqUiState.filters };
    }
    else if (button.hasAttribute('data-sq-window')) Object.assign(sqUiState.draft, core.windowRange(Number(button.dataset.sqWindow), 'native'), { salesMode: 'native' });
    else if (button.dataset.sqPreset === 'missing') { sqUiState.draft = { ...defaults(), scope: 'active', evidenceMode: 'missing' }; sqUiState.filters = { ...sqUiState.draft }; }
    else {
      Object.assign(sqUiState.draft, core.windowRange(30, 'native'), { scope: 'active', salesMode: 'native', productType: 'regular', salesMin: 0, salesMax: 5,
        availableMin: 1, availableMax: '', marginBasis: 'operating', marginMin: button.dataset.sqPreset === 'margin20' ? 20 : 15, marginMax: '',
        evidenceMode: sqUiState.draft.evidenceMode === 'missing' ? 'current' : sqUiState.draft.evidenceMode });
      sqUiState.filters = { ...sqUiState.draft };
    }
    sqUiState.page = 0; sqUiState.error = ''; sqRenderQuery();
  }, true);
  sqUiState.draft = { ...defaults() }; sqUiState.filters = { ...defaults() }; sqUiState.page = 0;
  const api = { ready: true, version: core.version, coverage: core.coverage, packetMeta: core.meta.historicalCoverage, resolveMode, periodCoverage, getRows, query: sqQuery, salesFor,
    normalizeStore: (platform, store) => core.storeName(platform, store).toLowerCase(), datasetRows: core.sourceRows, inScope: core.inScope, defaults,
    nearRange: days => core.windowRange(days, 'native'), exportResult: () => sqUiExport(), clearCaches: () => { aggregateCache.clear(); periodCache.clear(); } };
  core.query = api; root.spQueryPeriodModel = api; root.qpModel = api;
})(window);

</script>
<script>
/* 新来源的取消、测试、金额资格与未知数量兼容；不把用途未核的订单画成0。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  if (root.freshStatusModel?.ready) return;
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const fresh = row => Boolean(row.salesRefreshVersion || row.salesRefreshDataset);
  const excluded = row => row.cancelled || row.technicalTest || row.businessTest;
  const quantityUnknown = row => fresh(row) && !excluded(row) && !finite(row.quantity);
  const pendingRows = rows => rows.filter(quantityUnknown).length;
  const priorAmount = cxAmountEligible;
  cxAmountEligible = function (row) { return fresh(row) ? finite(row.amount) && row.amountChartEligible === true && !excluded(row) : priorAmount(row); };
  const priorMetrics = sxMetrics;
  sxMetrics = function (rows) {
    const result = priorMetrics(rows);
    if (!sxAnnual()) return result;
    const pending = pendingRows(rows), known = rows.length - pending;
    return { ...result, units: pending && !known ? null : result.units,
      amount: pending && !result.amountLines ? null : result.amount,
      quantityPendingRows: pending, quantityKnownRows: known, quantityComplete: pending === 0 };
  };
  const priorOverviewMetrics = ovMetrics;
  ovMetrics = function (rows = sxOrders()) {
    const result = priorOverviewMetrics(rows), metrics = sxMetrics(rows);
    return sxAnnual() ? { ...result, units: metrics.units, amount: metrics.quantityPendingRows && !metrics.amountLines ? null : result.amount,
      quantityPendingRows: metrics.quantityPendingRows, quantityKnownRows: metrics.quantityKnownRows, quantityComplete: metrics.quantityComplete } : result;
  };
  const priorAggregates = cxSalesAggregates;
  cxSalesAggregates = function (rows) {
    const result = priorAggregates(rows);
    if (!sxAnnual()) return result;
    const counts = new Map();
    for (const row of rows) {
      if (!counts.has(row.sku)) counts.set(row.sku, { total: 0, pending: 0 });
      const count = counts.get(row.sku); count.total++; if (quantityUnknown(row)) count.pending++;
    }
    for (const [sku, value] of result) {
      const count = counts.get(sku);
      if (count?.pending) Object.assign(value, { net: count.pending === count.total ? null : value.net, quantityPendingRows: count.pending });
    }
    return result;
  };
  const priorSrMetrics = srMetrics;
  srMetrics = function (rows, annual = sxAnnual()) {
    const result = priorSrMetrics(rows, annual), pending = annual ? pendingRows(rows) : 0;
    return pending ? { ...result, units: pending === rows.length ? null : result.units, upper: null,
      amount: result.amountKnown ? result.amount : null, quantityPendingRows: pending } : result;
  };
  const priorBreakdown = srBreakdown;
  srBreakdown = function (row) {
    const count = sxAnnual() ? pendingRows(row.rows || []) : 0;
    return priorBreakdown(row) + (count ? cxSmall(count + '行数量／用途待核，已有数量仅为小计') : '');
  };
  const priorRank = biRankHtml;
  biRankHtml = function () {
    let html = priorRank.apply(this, arguments);
    if (!sxAnnual()) return html;
    const values = cxSalesAggregates(sxOrders({ ignoreSku: true })), bySku = new Map([...values].map(([sku, value]) => [cxEsc(sku), value]));
    let unknown = 0;
    for (const value of values.values()) if (value.quantityPendingRows && value.net === null) unknown++;
    html = html.replace(/<div\b([^>]*class="bi-rank-row[^>]*>)([\s\S]*?)<\/div>/g, (tag, attributes) => {
      const value = bySku.get(attr(attributes, 'data-bi-sku'));
      if (!value?.quantityPendingRows) return tag;
      if (value.net === null) return '';
      return tag.replace(/(<span class="bi-rank-value">)([^<]+)(<\/span>)/, '$1≥$2$3')
        .replace(/当前范围[^";；]*套/, '已知参考小计' + cxQty(value.net) + '，另' + value.quantityPendingRows + '行数量／用途待核');
    });
    return html + (unknown ? '<p class="cx-caption fsc-rank-note">' + unknown + '个商品码数量待核，未参与销量排名。</p>' : '');
  };
  const priorDownload = cxDownloadCsv;
  cxDownloadCsv = function (filename, headers, rows) {
    if (sxAnnual() && String(filename).startsWith('商品销量_')) {
      const skuColumn = headers.indexOf('商品码'), values = new Map(srSalesRows().map(row => [row.sku, row.quantityPendingRows || 0]));
      if (skuColumn >= 0) {
        headers = [...headers.map(label => label.replace(/商品额/g, '源销售额参考')), '数量用途待核行数', '金额口径'];
        rows = rows.map(row => [...row, values.get(row[skuColumn]) || 0, root.salesRefreshModel.sourceLabel]);
      }
    }
    return priorDownload(filename, headers, rows);
  };
  const priorOverview = ovOverviewHtml;
  ovOverviewHtml = function (context) {
    let html = priorOverview(context);
    if (!sxAnnual()) return html;
    const metrics = sxMetrics(context.orders), count = metrics.quantityPendingRows;
    if (!count) return html;
    const note = '已知小计；' + count + '行数量／用途待核，不当作0';
    const card = (key, label, value, detail) => ovKpi(key, label, value, detail, { title: '来源状态或付款／用途证据未闭合，完整数量仍待核。' });
    html = html.replace(/<div class="ov-kpi [^"]*" data-ov-kpi="units"[\s\S]*?<\/div>/,
      card('units', '历史参考数量', finite(metrics.units) ? '≥' + cxQty(metrics.units) : '待核', note));
    html = html.replace(/<div class="ov-kpi [^"]*" data-ov-kpi="amount"[\s\S]*?<\/div>/,
      card('amount', '源销售额参考', metrics.amountLines ? cxMoney(metrics.amount) : '待核',
        (metrics.amountLines ? '已知小计；' : '') + count + '行数量／用途或付款待核，未计销售额'));
    return html;
  };

  const priorBuild = sqBuildRow;
  function queryNote(row) {
    if (row.salesMode !== 'annual') return row;
    const count = pendingRows(row._salesRows || []);
    return count ? { ...row, salesKnown: false, unitsUpper: null, periodQuantityPendingRows: count,
      coverageNote: row.coverageNote + '；' + count + '行数量／用途待核，已知数量为小计，未知不作0' } : row;
  }
  sqBuildRow = function () { return queryNote(priorBuild.apply(this, arguments)); };
  const priorCell = sqUiCell;
  sqUiCell = function (column, row) {
    let html = priorCell(column, row);
    if (column === 'units' && row.periodQuantityPendingRows) html = html.replace('本期未见计量证据', '源订单数量／用途待核')
      + sqUiSmall(row.periodQuantityPendingRows + '行数量／用途待核；不是0销量');
    return html;
  };
  const query = root.spQueryPeriodModel;
  if (query?.salesFor) {
    const priorSalesFor = query.salesFor;
    query.salesFor = function () {
      const result = priorSalesFor.apply(this, arguments), count = result.period.mode === 'annual' ? pendingRows(result.rows) : 0;
      return count ? { ...result, salesKnown: false, unitsUpper: null, quantityPendingRows: count,
        note: result.note + '；' + count + '行数量／用途待核，已有数量仅为小计' } : result;
    };
  }

  const attr = (attributes, key) => new RegExp('(?:^|\\s)' + key + '="([^"]*)"').exec(attributes)?.[1] || '';
  const hasClass = (attributes, key) => attr(attributes, 'class').split(/\s+/).includes(key);
  const keyForDate = (date, grain) => grain === 'month' ? date.slice(0, 7) : grain === 'week' ? sxAdd(date, -new Date(date + 'T00:00:00Z').getUTCDay()) : date;
  function trendModel(rows, options, measure = 'units') {
    const groups = options.groups || sxPlatforms, grain = options.grain || 'month';
    const buckets = sxTrendBuckets(rows, options), map = new Map(buckets.map(bucket => [bucket.key, { ...bucket, known: 0, pending: 0, total: 0, bySeries: new Map() }]));
    for (const row of rows) {
      const bucket = map.get(keyForDate(row.date, grain));
      if (!bucket?.covered || row.date < bucket.coveredStart || row.date > bucket.coveredEnd) continue;
      const key = String(row.platform || '');
      if (!bucket.bySeries.has(key)) bucket.bySeries.set(key, { known: 0, pending: 0, total: 0 });
      const series = bucket.bySeries.get(key), unknown = measure === 'units' ? quantityUnknown(row) : fresh(row) && !excluded(row) && !cxAmountEligible(row);
      if (unknown) { series.pending++; bucket.pending++; continue; }
      const value = excluded(row) ? 0 : measure === 'amount' ? cxAmountEligible(row) ? row.amount : 0 : (options.valueFn || cxNet)(row);
      if (!finite(value)) continue;
      series.known++; series.total += value; bucket.known++; bucket.total += value;
    }
    const hidden = new Set(), hiddenTotals = new Set();
    for (const bucket of map.values()) {
      if (!bucket.pending) continue;
      if (!bucket.known) { bucket.total = null; hiddenTotals.add(bucket.key); }
      for (const group of groups) {
        const series = bucket.bySeries.get(group.key);
        if (hiddenTotals.has(bucket.key) || series?.pending && !series.known) hidden.add(bucket.key + '|' + group.key);
      }
    }
    return { map, buckets: [...map.values()], groups, hidden, hiddenTotals, pending: [...map.values()].reduce((sum, bucket) => sum + bucket.pending, 0) };
  }
  function trendHtml(html, options, model, measure) {
    if (!model.pending) return html;
    const action = options.actionAttribute || 'data-sx-bucket', centers = new Map();
    html.replace(/<g\b([^>]*class="sx-trend-bucket[^>]*>)([\s\S]*?)<\/g>/g, (tag, attributes, content) => {
      const hit = content.match(/<rect\b([^>]*class="sx-trend-hit"[^>]*)>/);
      if (hit) centers.set(Math.round((Number(attr(hit[1], 'x')) + Number(attr(hit[1], 'width')) / 2) * 10), attr(attributes, action));
      return tag;
    });
    const keyForX = x => centers.get(Math.round(Number(x) * 10));
    const split = (points, series) => {
      const segments = []; let current = [];
      for (const point of points) {
        if (model.hidden.has(keyForX(point.split(',')[0]) + '|' + series)) { if (current.length > 1) segments.push(current); current = []; }
        else current.push(point);
      }
      if (current.length > 1) segments.push(current);
      return segments;
    };
    html = html.replace(/<(circle|text|line|rect)\b([^>]*)>[\s\S]*?<\/\1>/g, (tag, name, attributes) => {
      const key = attr(attributes, action), series = attr(attributes, 'data-sx-series');
      if (model.hidden.has(key + '|' + series) && ['sx-trend-point', 'sx-trend-point-label', 'sx-trend-point-guide', 'sx-trend-bar', 'sx-trend-segment-label'].some(value => hasClass(attributes, value))) return '';
      if (model.hiddenTotals.has(key) && hasClass(attributes, 'sx-trend-total-label')) return '';
      if (hasClass(attributes, 'sx-trend-line-hit') && (model.hidden.has(key + '|' + series) || model.hidden.has(keyForX(attr(attributes, 'x1')) + '|' + series))) return '';
      if (measure === 'units' && model.map.get(key)?.pending && hasClass(attributes, 'sx-trend-total-label')) return tag.replace(/>([^<]+)<\/text>$/, '>≥$1</text>');
      if (measure === 'units' && model.map.get(key)?.bySeries.get(series)?.pending && ['sx-trend-point-label', 'sx-trend-segment-label'].some(value => hasClass(attributes, value))) return tag.replace(/>([^<]+)<\/text>$/, '>≥$1</text>');
      return tag;
    });
    html = html.replace(/<polyline\b([^>]*)>[\s\S]*?<\/polyline>/g, (tag, attributes) => hasClass(attributes, 'sx-trend-line')
      ? split(attr(attributes, 'points').trim().split(/\s+/), attr(attributes, 'data-sx-series')).map(points => tag.replace(/points="[^"]*"/, 'points="' + points.join(' ') + '"')).join('') : tag);
    html = html.replace(/<g\b([^>]*)>([\s\S]*?)<\/g>/g, (tag, attributes, content) => {
      if (!hasClass(attributes, 'sx-trend-tracer')) return tag;
      const motion = content.match(/<animateMotion\b[^>]*path="([^"]*)"/);
      return motion ? split(motion[1].replace(/^M/, '').split(/\s+L/), attr(attributes, 'data-sx-series'))
        .map(points => tag.replace(/path="[^"]*"/, 'path="M' + points.join(' L') + '"')).join('') : tag;
    });
    const format = value => measure === 'amount' ? cxMoney(value) : cxQty(value), unit = measure === 'amount' ? '金额' : '数量／用途';
    html = html.replace(/<g\b([^>]*class="sx-trend-bucket[^>]*>)([\s\S]*?)<\/g>/g, (tag, attributes, content) => {
      const bucket = model.map.get(attr(attributes, action));
      if (!bucket?.pending) return tag;
      const title = bucket.label + '；' + (bucket.known ? '已知小计 ' + format(bucket.total) : unit + '待核') + '；' + model.groups.map(group => {
        const series = bucket.bySeries.get(group.key);
        return (group.label || group.key) + ' ' + (series?.pending ? (series.known ? '≥' + format(series.total) + '，' : '') + series.pending + '行待核' : format(series?.total || 0));
      }).join('；') + '；' + bucket.pending + '行' + unit + '待核，未知不作0';
      return '<g' + attributes.replace(/aria-label="[^"]*"/, 'aria-label="' + cxEsc(title) + '"')
        + content.replace(/<title>[\s\S]*?<\/title>/, '<title>' + cxEsc(title) + '</title>')
          .replace(model.hiddenTotals.has(bucket.key) ? /<line class="sx-trend-empty-bar"[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/line>/g : /$^/, '') + '</g>';
    });
    const viewBox = html.match(/viewBox="0 0 ([\d.]+) ([\d.]+)"/), height = Number(viewBox?.[2] || 310);
    const markers = [...centers].filter(([, key]) => model.map.get(key)?.pending).map(([x, key]) => '<text class="fsc-pending-marker" ' + action + '="' + cxEsc(key) + '" x="' + (x / 10).toFixed(2) + '" y="' + (height - 61) + '" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" fill="#65778e">待核' + model.map.get(key).pending + '</text>').join('');
    html = html.replace('</svg>', markers + '</svg>');
    return html + '<p class="cx-caption fsc-status-note">已知' + (measure === 'amount' ? '金额' : '数量') + '仅为来源内小计；' + model.pending + '行' + unit + '待核，未知部分不显示为0。</p>';
  }
  const priorTrend = sxTrendHtml;
  sxTrendHtml = function (rows, options = {}) {
    let html = priorTrend(rows, options);
    const action = options.actionAttribute || 'data-sx-bucket';
    if (!sxAnnual() || !/^data-sx-(?:detail-)?bucket$/.test(action)) return html;
    const measure = action === 'data-sx-detail-bucket' ? root.faUiModel?.state.detailMeasure : root.faUiModel?.state.measure;
    return trendHtml(html, options, trendModel(rows, options, measure === 'amount' ? 'amount' : 'units'), measure === 'amount' ? 'amount' : 'units');
  };
  root.freshStatusModel = { ready: true, quantityUnknown, pendingRows, trendModel, trendHtml, queryNote };
})(window);

</script>
<script>
/* 新品监控更新独立状态保留参考；原np补货计算和清仓审批快照保持原输入。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const core = root.salesRefreshModel; if (!core?.query?.ready) throw Error('销量查询未初始化');
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const originalMonitor = new Map(nmData.rows.map(row => [row.sku, row]));
  const cutoff = core.meta.monitorEnd || core.coverage.annual.end;
  const monitorRows = (core.data.monitorRows || []).filter(row => originalMonitor.has(row.sku) && row.date <= cutoff);
  const bySku = new Map(); for (const row of monitorRows) { if (!bySku.has(row.sku)) bySku.set(row.sku, []); bySku.get(row.sku).push(row); }
  function stats(rows) {
    let lower = 0, upper = 0, upperKnown = true;
    for (const row of rows) { if (finite(row.netReferenceLower)) lower += row.netReferenceLower; if (finite(row.netReferenceUpper)) upper += row.netReferenceUpper; else upperKnown = false; }
    return { rows: rows.length, lower, upper: upperKnown ? upper : null };
  }
  function windowRows(sku, days) { const start = sxAdd(cutoff, 1 - days); return (bySku.get(sku) || []).filter(row => row.date >= start && row.date <= cutoff); }
  for (const original of originalMonitor.values()) {
    const rows = bySku.get(original.sku) || [], retained = rows.filter(row => !row.excluded && row.netReferenceLower > 0), dates = retained.map(row => row.date).sort();
    const pending = rows.some(row => !row.excluded && (row.partialRefundPending || row.statusUnknown || row.identityPending || row.countryPending || !finite(row.netReferenceUpper) || row.netReferenceLower !== row.netReferenceUpper));
    const orderState = retained.length ? 'observed' : pending ? 'pending' : rows.some(row => row.fullRefundOrReturned) ? 'returned' : rows.length ? 'no_retained' : 'no_rows';
    const recent30 = stats(windowRows(original.sku, 30)), recent60 = stats(windowRows(original.sku, 60));
    const arrived = original.firstKnownArrival ? rows.filter(row => row.date >= original.firstKnownArrival) : [];
    const days = original.firstKnownArrival ? Math.floor((sqDateNumber(cutoff) - sqDateNumber(original.firstKnownArrival)) / 86400000) : null;
    const updated = { ...original, observedCumulative: stats(rows), sinceArrivalObserved: stats(arrived), sales30Lower: recent30.lower, sales30Upper: recent30.upper,
      sales60Lower: recent60.lower, sales60Upper: recent60.upper, arrivalDaysAtCutoff: finite(days) && days >= 0 ? days : null,
      firstObservedRetainedOrder: dates[0] || null, lastObservedRetainedOrder: dates.at(-1) || null, orderState, orderLabel: nmOrderLabels[orderState],
      cumulativeStart: original.firstKnownArrival || rows.map(row => row.date).sort()[0] || null,
      cumulativeLabel: original.firstKnownArrival ? '到仓日后源内状态保留参考（非完整生命周期净量）' : '源内已见状态保留参考（到仓日待核）',
      salesRefreshVersion: core.version };
    const projected = root.faNewInventoryModel?.projected ? root.faNewInventoryModel.projected(updated) : updated;
    nmBySku.set(original.sku, projected);
  }
  nmData.rows.splice(0, nmData.rows.length, ...[...originalMonitor.keys()].map(sku => nmBySku.get(sku)));
  nmData.meta.salesCutoff = cutoff;
  nmData.meta.freshness = '状态保留参考至' + cutoff + '；未覆盖日期不作0，原拟定补货保留方案日期。';
  for (const source of core.meta.sources || []) if (source.file === '产品销量.xlsx' && !nmData.sources.some(old => old.sha256 === source.sha256))
    nmData.sources.unshift({ path: source.file, sha256: source.sha256, meaning: '本轮最新状态保留参考；原数量及排除证据逐行保留' });
  nmDetailRecords = function (row, ignoreBucket = false) {
    const start = nmState.detailWindow === '30' ? sxAdd(cutoff, -29) : nmState.detailWindow === '60' ? sxAdd(cutoff, -59) : row.firstKnownArrival || '';
    return (bySku.get(row.sku) || []).filter(order => (!start || order.date >= start) && order.date <= cutoff
      && (ignoreBucket || !nmState.detailBucket || order.date.slice(0, 7) === nmState.detailBucket)).sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date) || Number(b.sourceRow) - Number(a.sourceRow));
  };
  nmDetailTrend = function (row) {
    const records = nmDetailRecords(row, true), start = nmState.detailWindow === '30' ? sxAdd(cutoff, -29) : nmState.detailWindow === '60' ? sxAdd(cutoff, -59)
      : row.firstKnownArrival || records.map(record => record.date).sort()[0];
    if (!start) return '<p class="nm-muted">当前没有可核的源内日期起点。</p>';
    return sxTrendHtml(records, { start, end: cutoff, coverageStart: start, coverageEnd: cutoff, grain: 'month', renderMode: 'bar', groups: sxPlatforms,
      valueFn: order => order.excluded ? 0 : order.netReferenceLower || 0, pointLabels: false, pulseEnabled: false, width: 850, height: 260,
      selectedBucket: nmState.detailBucket, title: '三平台 · 状态保留参考（月）', actionAttribute: 'data-nm-detail-bucket' })
      + '<p class="nm-muted">状态保留参考至' + cxEsc(cutoff) + '，不作为全生命周期净量。</p>';
  };
  function short(date) { return date.slice(5).replace('-', '/').replace(/^0/, ''); }
  function decorateMonitor(host) {
    if (!host) return;
    const replace = text => text.replace(/11\/11–11\/13的销量尚未覆盖。/g, '销量参考至' + short(cutoff) + '；测算保留原方案日期。')
      .replace(/10\/12—11\/10/g, short(sxAdd(cutoff, -29)) + '—' + short(cutoff)).replace(/9\/12—11\/10/g, short(sxAdd(cutoff, -59)) + '—' + short(cutoff))
      .replace(/截至11\/10/g, '截至' + short(cutoff)).replace(/—11\/10/g, '—' + short(cutoff));
    if (typeof document.createTreeWalker === 'function') { const walker = document.createTreeWalker(host, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT); let node; while (node = walker.nextNode()) node.textContent = replace(node.textContent); }
    if (!host.querySelector('.srf-monitor-source')) host.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', '<p class="cx-caption srf-monitor-source">销量参考至' + cxEsc(cutoff) + '；测算保留原方案日期。</p>');
  }
  const beforeMonitor = nmRender; nmRender = function () { const result = beforeMonitor(); decorateMonitor(cxEl('cxNewsBody')); return result; };
  const beforeMonitorDetail = nmRenderSku; nmRenderSku = function () { const result = beforeMonitorDetail(); decorateMonitor(cxEl('cxDetailBody')); return result; };
  const beforeMonitorHeader = nmHeader; nmHeader = function () {
    const result = beforeMonitorHeader(); if (cxState.view === 'newproducts' && nmState.mode === 'monitor') cxEl('subtitle').textContent = '新品状态保留销量参考至' + cutoff + ' · 到仓证据、库存及原测算沿各自日期'; return result;
  };
  function latestHtml(sku) {
    const thirty = core.skuWindow(sku, 30), sixty = core.skuWindow(sku, 60);
    return '<div class="srf-latest-sales"><b>最新30／60天参考：' + cxRange(thirty.lower, thirty.upper) + ' / ' + cxRange(sixty.lower, sixty.upper)
      + '</b><small>至' + cxEsc(core.coverage.annual.end) + ' · 同SKU全部已见订单参考，非净量</small></div>';
  }
  const beforeLeaderSales = leaderSalesHtml;
  leaderSalesHtml = function (row) { return latestHtml(row.sku) + '<details><summary>原方案净量快照</summary>' + beforeLeaderSales(row) + '</details>'; };
  const beforeSkuOverview = cxSkuOverview;
  cxSkuOverview = function (sku) { return latestHtml(sku) + beforeSkuOverview(sku); };
  function parseCsv(value) {
    const result = [], text = String(value).replace(/^\ufeff/, ''); let row = [], cell = '', quoted = false;
    for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index++) {
      const char = text[index];
      if (char === '"') { if (quoted && text[index + 1] === '"') { cell += '"'; index++; } else quoted = !quoted; }
      else if (!quoted && char === ',') { row.push(cell); cell = ''; }
      else if (!quoted && (char === '\n' || char === '\r')) { if (char === '\r' && text[index + 1] === '\n') index++; row.push(cell); result.push(row); row = []; cell = ''; }
      else cell += char;
    }
    if (cell || row.length) { row.push(cell); result.push(row); } return result;
  }
  const beforeLeaderCsv = leaderCsv;
  leaderCsv = function () {
    const originalDownload = downloadManual;
    downloadManual = function (value, filename, type) {
      if (!String(filename).startsWith('降价审批清单_')) return originalDownload(value, filename, type);
      const rows = parseCsv(value), headers = ['最新参考截止', '最新30天参考下界', '最新30天参考上界', '最新60天参考下界', '最新60天参考上界', '参考口径'];
      const output = rows.map((row, index) => {
        if (!index) return [...row, ...headers];
        const thirty = core.skuWindow(row[0], 30), sixty = core.skuWindow(row[0], 60);
        return [...row, core.coverage.annual.end, thirty.lower, thirty.upper, sixty.lower, sixty.upper, '同SKU已见历史订单参考；原方案及净量快照不改'];
      });
      const quote = entry => '"' + String(entry ?? '').replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
      return originalDownload('\ufeff' + output.map(row => row.map(quote).join(',')).join('\r\n'), filename, type);
    };
    try { return beforeLeaderCsv(); } finally { downloadManual = originalDownload; }
  };
  const priorDownload = cxDownloadCsv;
  cxDownloadCsv = function (filename, headers, rows) {
    if (String(filename).startsWith('新品出单监控_')) filename = '新品出单监控_资料至' + cutoff.replace(/-/g, '') + '.csv';
    return priorDownload(filename, headers, rows);
  };
  core.monitor = { ready: true, cutoff, rows: monitorRows, bySku, stats, windowRows, originalMonitor, latestHtml };
  core.finish();
})(window);

</script>

</script>

<script>
/* 日期快捷入口按美西业务今天取值，来源覆盖仍由销量模型判断。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  if (root.monthTodayUiModel?.ready) return;
  if (!root.salesRefreshModel?.runtimeReady) throw Error('月初至今天快捷入口需要销量模型');
  const timeZone = 'America/Los_Angeles';
  const formatter = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone, year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' });
  let selected = '';
  function todayRange(instant = new Date()) {
    const fields = Object.fromEntries(formatter.formatToParts(new Date(instant)).map(part => [part.type, part.value]));
    const end = fields.year + '-' + fields.month + '-' + fields.day;
    return { start: end.slice(0, 7) + '-01', end };
  }
  function presetRange(key, dataset) {
    if (key === 'monthToday') return todayRange();
    const coverage = root.salesRefreshModel.getCoverage(key === 'year' ? 'annual' : dataset);
    return key === 'year' ? { start: coverage.start.slice(0, 4) + '-01-01', end: coverage.start.slice(0, 4) + '-12-31', dataset: 'annual' }
      : { start: sxAdd(coverage.end, 1 - Number(key)), end: coverage.end };
  }
  function read() {
    return { start: cxEl('sxDateStart')?.value, end: cxEl('sxDateEnd')?.value, dataset: cxEl('sxDateDataset')?.value };
  }
  function matches(key, range) {
    const expected = presetRange(key, range.dataset);
    return (!expected.dataset || range.dataset === expected.dataset) && range.start === expected.start && range.end === expected.end;
  }
  function sync() {
    const dialog = cxEl('sxDateDialog'), buttons = [...dialog?.querySelectorAll('[data-mtt-preset]') || []];
    if (!buttons.length) return;
    const range = read(), keys = buttons.map(button => button.dataset.mttPreset);
    if (!keys.includes(selected) || !matches(selected, range)) selected = keys.find(key => matches(key, range)) || '';
    for (const button of buttons) {
      const pressed = String(button.dataset.mttPreset === selected);
      if (button.getAttribute('aria-pressed') !== pressed) button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', pressed);
      button.classList.add('fa-state-control', 'mtt-state-control');
      if (button.dataset.mttPreset === 'monthToday') {
        const today = todayRange();
        button.title = '美西业务日期（America/Los_Angeles）：' + today.start + '—' + today.end + '；未覆盖日期保留待核';
      }
    }
  }
  function install() {
    const dialog = cxEl('sxDateDialog'), host = dialog?.querySelector('.sx-date-presets');
    if (!host) return;
    // 旧快捷监听会停止事件传播；只转换弹窗按钮属性，统一管理其互斥选中态。
    for (const button of host.querySelectorAll('[data-srf-preset],[data-sx-preset]')) {
      button.dataset.mttPreset = button.dataset.srfPreset || button.dataset.sxPreset;
      button.removeAttribute('data-srf-preset'); button.removeAttribute('data-sx-preset');
    }
    if (!host.querySelector('[data-mtt-preset="monthToday"]')) {
      const button = document.createElement('button'); button.type = 'button'; button.dataset.mttPreset = 'monthToday';
      button.textContent = '月初至今天'; host.prepend(button);
    }
    sync();
  }
  const priorDialog = sxDateDialog;
  sxDateDialog = function () { const result = priorDialog(); install(); return result; };
  root.addEventListener('click', event => {
    const button = event.target.closest('#sxDateDialog [data-mtt-preset]'); if (!button) return;
    event.preventDefault(); event.stopImmediatePropagation();
    selected = button.dataset.mttPreset;
    const dataset = cxEl('sxDateDataset'), range = presetRange(selected, dataset.value);
    if (range.dataset) dataset.value = range.dataset;
    cxEl('sxDateStart').value = range.start; cxEl('sxDateEnd').value = range.end;
    cxEl('sxMonth').value = range.end.slice(0, 7);
    cxEl('sxDateError').textContent = '';
    sxDateHint(); sync();
  }, true);
  function manual(event) {
    if (!['sxDateStart', 'sxDateEnd', 'sxDateDataset', 'sxMonth'].includes(event.target.id)) return;
    selected = '';
    Promise.resolve().then(sync);
  }
  document.addEventListener('input', manual, true); document.addEventListener('change', manual, true);
  root.monthTodayUiModel = { ready: true, timeZone, todayRange, presetRange, matches, install, sync };
})(window);

</script>

</script>
